![image](/images/Icon-Whip.png?8842d)

**Weapons** are used by the [character](/w/Character) to fend off [enemies](/w/Enemy), each having its own unique way of doing so. Most weapons deal damage to enemies, while some apply debuffs to enemies or buffs to the player.

The player can select up to six different weapons from level-ups, but it is possible to carry more by picking up weapons that [generate with the stage](/w/Passive_item#Stage_items). Most weapons must be unlocked before they can be offered to the player.

Most weapons are unique, which means a character can only gain a weapon once per run, and there is no way to get the same weapon multiple times (even if it is consumed). Exceptions include single-use weapons like [![image](/images/thumb/Sprite-Candybox.png/24px-Sprite-Candybox.png?f1d13)](/w/Candybox) [Candybox](/w/Candybox) or [![image](/images/thumb/Sprite-Arma_Dio.png/24px-Sprite-Arma_Dio.png?36163)](/w/Arma_Dio) [Arma Dio](/w/Arma_Dio).

## Obtaining

Normally, each character has access to one of the base weapons as their starting weapon and is able to obtain additional base weapons as they [level up](/w/Level_up). Rarity determines the [weight](/w/Level_up#Mechanics) of how often that weapon shows up in the pool of possible weapons and [passive items](/w/Passive_item) during the level-up screen and when opening [![image](/images/thumb/Sprite-Treasure_Chest.png/24px-Sprite-Treasure_Chest.png?a81fe)](/w/Treasure_Chest) [Treasure Chests](/w/Treasure_Chest).

### Upgrading

There is a certain [chance](/w/Level_up#Receiving_owned_items) for weapons that the player has already owned to be offered to them upon level-up. If an already owned weapon is selected, it will be upgraded to a higher level, and it will gain certain stat bonuses unique to the weapon. When a weapon is fully leveled, it cannot appear in the level-up options anymore. Upgrading a weapon will also reset its [![image](/images/Sprite-Cooldown.png?4db6a)](/w/Cooldown) [Cooldown](/w/Cooldown) and activate it instantly, which is useful for powerful weapons with long Cooldown.

### Evolution

When a base weapon is fully leveled and its corresponding passive item is obtained, it can be transformed into an evolved weapon by collecting a [![image](/images/thumb/Sprite-Treasure_Chest_2.png/24px-Sprite-Treasure_Chest_2.png?d086d)](/w/Treasure_Chest) [Treasure Chest](/w/Treasure_Chest). Some weapons only need to be fully leveled for them to evolve. Not all weapons have an evolution.

Evolution actually works by removing the base weapon and acquiring the evolved one (with a few exceptions), which means whenever a weapon is evolved, all the effects of the original weapon will be lost, and the evolved weapon's Cooldown starts from zero (which is noticeable for weapons with long Cooldown, like [Santa Water](/w/Santa_Water)). Evolved weapons also remove the base weapon regardless of whether obtained by normal evolution rules or not (for example, obtaining an evolution by [![image](/images/thumb/Sprite-Super_Candybox_II_Turbo.png/24px-Sprite-Super_Candybox_II_Turbo.png?c50b3)](/w/Super_Candybox_II_Turbo) [Super Candybox II Turbo](/w/Super_Candybox_II_Turbo) will also remove its base weapon).

Certain characters ( [![image](/images/thumb/Sprite-Mortaccio.png/24px-Sprite-Mortaccio.png?9e086)](/w/Mortaccio) [Mortaccio](/w/Mortaccio), [![image](/images/thumb/Sprite-Yatta_Cavallo.png/24px-Sprite-Yatta_Cavallo.png?7ca65)](/w/Yatta_Cavallo) [Yatta Cavallo](/w/Yatta_Cavallo), [![image](/images/thumb/Sprite-Bianca_Ramba.png/24px-Sprite-Bianca_Ramba.png?51305)](/w/Bianca_Ramba) [Bianca Ramba](/w/Bianca_Ramba), and [![image](/images/thumb/Sprite-O%27Sole_Meeo.png/24px-Sprite-O%27Sole_Meeo.png?51fcd)](/w/O%27Sole_Meeo) [O'Sole Meeo](/w/O%27Sole_Meeo)) automatically evolve their starting weapon when they morph into a new form, provided that the player has obtained the required [relic](/w/Relic) and met the necessary condition.

## Stats

Weapons have different stats, which define their functions.

See [Overview Stats](/w/Weapons/Overview_Stats) for a list of base stats of the weapons.

| Weapon Stats | Weapon Stats | Weapon Stats | Weapon Stats |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Max Level** | The highest level the weapon can be upgraded to. | **Rarity** | The weight of the weapon in the pool of weapons and passive items. |
| **Base Damage** | The damage dealt by a single projectile per hit. Modified by [Might](/w/Might) stat. [[1]](#cite_note-1) | **Area** | The base area of the weapon. Modified by [Area](/w/Area) stat. |
| **Speed** | The base projectile speed of the weapon. Modified by [Speed](/w/Speed) stat. | **Amount** | The amount of projectiles fired per use. Modified by [Amount](/w/Amount) stat. |
| **Duration** | The duration of the weapon's effect. Modified by [Duration](/w/Duration) stat. | **Pierce** | The number of enemies a single projectile can hit before being used up. |
| **Cooldown** | The time required for the weapon to be used again. There is a trigger for the cooldown to start after a weapon's duration ends. Modified by [Cooldown](/w/Cooldown) stat. | **Projectile Interval** | The time required for an additional projectile to be fired between each Cooldown. Can overlap with another set of shots even before it finishes shooting. |
| **Hitbox Delay** | The time before the same enemy can be hit by the same projectile again. All delays refresh simultaneously as soon as one fully counts down. | **Knockback** | The [knockback](/w/Knockback) dealt multiplier of the weapon. Determines the strength of the knockback effect along with the enemy's knockback taken multiplier and movement speed. |
| **Pool Limit** | The maximum amount of projectiles that can be on screen. | **Chance** | The chance of a special effect happening. Modified by [Luck](/w/Luck) stat. Technically, [Crit Chance](/w/Critical_Hit) is a separate stat but both have not been used at the same time yet. |
| **Crit Multi** | Damage multiplier applied to [critical hits](/w/Critical_Hit). | **Block by Walls** | Can the projectile be blocked by obstacles. |

1. [↑](#cite_ref-1) *The damage displayed with the "Damage Numbers" option ticked shows a randomized value within ±5 of the scaled base damage, but this is only a visual feature and the damage dealt by a weapon is always the same.*

## Types

There are base, [evolutions or unions](/w/Evolution), and special weapons.

See [Combos](/w/Weapons/Combos) for a list of which [passive items](/w/Passive_item) and their corresponding [player stats](/w/Player_stats) affect which weapon.

Certain weapons are counted as specific weapon type, which will interact with certain effects. This include:

- [![image](/images/thumb/Sprite-Fire_damage.png/24px-Sprite-Fire_damage.png?20c0a)](/w/Fire_damage) [Fire damage](/w/Fire_damage) weapons, which have synergy with [![image](/images/thumb/Sprite-Heir_of_Fate_%28XIX%29.png/17px-Sprite-Heir_of_Fate_%28XIX%29.png?aaf24)](/w/Heir_of_Fate_(XIX)) [Heir of Fate (XIX)](/w/Heir_of_Fate_(XIX)), [![image](/images/thumb/Sprite-Dario_Bossi.png/21px-Sprite-Dario_Bossi.png?799c9)](/w/Dario_Bossi) [Dario Bossi](/w/Dario_Bossi) [![This content requires the Ode to Castlevania DLC to access](/images/thumb/Sprite-DLCBadge-OdeToCastlevania.png/12px-Sprite-DLCBadge-OdeToCastlevania.png?10a42)](/w/Ode_to_Castlevania), [Whiteout](/w/Whiteout) and [The Coop](/w/The_Coop).
- [![image](/images/thumb/Sprite-Hell_damage.png/24px-Sprite-Hell_damage.png?fa9bc)](/w/Hell_damage) [Hell damage](/w/Hell_damage) weapons, which have synergy with [![image](/images/thumb/Sprite-Baal%27Thasar.png/24px-Sprite-Baal%27Thasar.png?f56a2)](/w/Baal%27Thasar) [Baal'Thasar](/w/Baal%27Thasar) [![This content requires the Legacy of the Bloodmoon DLC to access](/images/thumb/Sprite-DLCBadge-LegacyOfTheBloodmoon.png/12px-Sprite-DLCBadge-LegacyOfTheBloodmoon.png?849b0)](/w/Legacy_of_the_Bloodmoon).
- [![image](/images/thumb/Sprite-Blood_damage.png/24px-Sprite-Blood_damage.png?146de)](/w/Blood_damage) [Blood damage](/w/Blood_damage) weapons, which have synergy with [![image](/images/thumb/Sprite-Megalo_Syuuto_Moonspell.png/24px-Sprite-Megalo_Syuuto_Moonspell.png?ad4e9)](/w/Megalo_Syuuto_Moonspell) [Megalo Syuuto Moonspell](/w/Megalo_Syuuto_Moonspell) [![This content requires the Legacy of the Moonspell DLC to access](/images/thumb/Sprite-DLCBadge-LegacyOfTheMoonSpell.png/12px-Sprite-DLCBadge-LegacyOfTheMoonSpell.png?81d28)](/w/Legacy_of_the_Moonspell) and [![image](/images/thumb/Sprite-Baal%27Thasar.png/24px-Sprite-Baal%27Thasar.png?f56a2)](/w/Baal%27Thasar) [Baal'Thasar](/w/Baal%27Thasar) [![This content requires the Legacy of the Bloodmoon DLC to access](/images/thumb/Sprite-DLCBadge-LegacyOfTheBloodmoon.png/12px-Sprite-DLCBadge-LegacyOfTheBloodmoon.png?849b0)](/w/Legacy_of_the_Bloodmoon).

Despite not listed as a weapon type, certain weapons have [Freeze](/w/Freeze) effect and synergies with various [Arcanas](/w/Arcanas) such as [![image](/images/thumb/Sprite-Crystal_Cries_%28XII%29.png/17px-Sprite-Crystal_Cries_%28XII%29.png?6a5e9)](/w/Crystal_Cries_(XII)) [Crystal Cries (XII)](/w/Crystal_Cries_(XII)).

### Evolutions

Traditionally, evolved weapons cannot be upgraded, only have one level, and have a rarity of 1. However, newer expansion content such as the [Emerald Diorama](/w/Emerald_Diorama) breaks many of the older conventions for weapon evolutions. Notable exceptions of older weapons that broke these conventions are the Bi-Bracelet, Tri-Bracelet, Alucard Swords, Vol Confodere, Melio Confodere, Vol Luminatio, and Vol Umbra. Many newer weapons also have additional secondary evolutions.

### Unions

Union weapons are a unique type of evolution. They merge two weapons into one and free up a weapon slot. They may or may not need a passive item to be united.

### Gifts

[Gifts](/w/Gifts) are extra weapons or [passive items](/w/Passive_item) which can only be obtained via [Treasure Chest](/w/Treasure_Chest) when player meets certain conditions. Most gifts do not replace the base weapon or [passive item](/w/Passive_item).

### Special Weapons

Special weapons cannot appear when leveling up. They are used as the structure of an attack that is temporary or an effect. When they are used as an effect, they behave slightly differently compared to when they are modified to be actual weapons.

## Update history

This information has been manually compiled. Some updates may not be included yet.

- [update](/w/Updates) **17 August 2023 ( [Update](/w/Updates/v1.6.0_patch_notes)):** - Fixed hidden weapons not being checked when activating some arcanas.
- Weapon Selection: Header size adjustment.

- [update](/w/Updates) **1 June 2023 ( [Update](/w/Updates/1.4.200_-_Bugfixes_%26_tweaks)):** - Fix for level up evo indicators not showing on passives for DLC weapons
- Fix for issue where pick-up weapons could not be leveled up

- [update](/w/Updates) **21 July 2022 ( [Update](/w/Updates/Patch_0.9.0)):** - Added a cap to Duration for Limit Broken weapons
- Fixed some projectiles standing still when there are no enemies

- [update](/w/Updates) **12 April 2022 ( [Update](/w/Updates/Patch_0.5.0)):** - Removed special BGM from weapons (it stays on special characters only)

- [update](/w/Updates) **25 March 2022 ( [Update](/w/Updates/Patch_0.3.2)):** - Added info for discovered evolutions on Level Up panels

- [update](/w/Updates) **12 March 2022 ( [Update](/w/Updates/Patch_0.3.1)):** - changes to cooldown don't reset weapon firing timers anymore

- [update](/w/Updates) **13 February 2022 ( [Update](/w/Updates/Patch_0.2.12_-_small_update)):** - Minor tweaks to weapons rarity

- [update](/w/Updates) **16 January 2022 ( [Update](/w/Updates/Patch_0.2.8_-_Cloud_Saves,_Volume_Sliders,_Stats_Display)):** - after unlocking a handful of weapons, current stats and weapon levels will be displayed when leveling up and on the pause screen

- [update](/w/Updates) **7 January 2022 ( [Update](/w/Updates/Patch_0.2.6b:_fullscreen,_skip_animations,_DPS_stats)):** - tentative fix to slow downs with certain weapon combos
- adjusted tips for a few weapons if there's any stat they ignore

- [update](/w/Updates) **27 October 2021 ( [Update](/w/Updates/There_exist_tales_that_don%27t_exist.)):** - Added to game.

## Trivia

- Before Ode to Castlevania DLC, [Bi-Bracelet](/w/Bi-Bracelet) and [Tri-Bracelet](/w/Tri-Bracelet) were the only evolutions that have a rarity of more than 1.
 - [Fuwalafuwaloo](/w/Fuwalafuwaloo) used to have a rarity of 50, same as [Vento Sacro](/w/Vento_Sacro); it changed to 1 in a later patch.
